A shallow repair (prefer a non-null sample) still breaks for a metric that is empty in every row.\r\n3. Anything created after\n# the harbor_base commit (installed dependencies, build or test output) is untracked, and a\n# bare `add -A` would sweep it into the patch. Stage tracked modifications, then add only\n# untracked files that LOOK like source -- this keeps the agent's work and drops generated\n# output by default.\n_SUB=/logs/verifier/submission.patch\n_SUBIDX=/tmp/.submission_index\n_SUBSRC=('*.c' '*.cc' '*.cpp' '*.cxx' '*.h' '*.hh' '*.hpp' '*.hxx' '*.inl' '*.m' '*.mm'\n         '*.py' '*.rb' '*.go' '*.rs' '*.java' '*.js' '*.ts' '*.tsx' '*.cs' '*.kt' '*.swift')\nrm -f \"$_SUBIDX\"\nGIT_INDEX_FILE=\"$_SUBIDX\" git -C /app read-tree harbor_base 2>>\"$STDERR_LOG\" || true\nGIT_INDEX_FILE=\"$_SUBIDX\" git -C /app add -u 2>>\"$STDERR_LOG\" || true\n# Enumerate before adding: `git add` aborts and stages NOTHING if any one pathspec matches\n# no file, which silently drops the agent's new files on most tasks.\n# Generated-output dirs are dropped from the UNTRACKED scan only. A project that keeps real\n# source under lib/ is unaffected: those files are tracked at harbor_base, so `add -u` above\n# already has them. This drops only files CREATED under a build dir during the run -- which,\n# left in, break replay: they are recorded as new-file hunks and a fresh container from the\n# same image already has them, so `git apply` fails with \"already exists in working directory\".\nmapfile -t _SUBNEW < <(git -C /app ls-files --others --exclude-standard \\\n  -- \"${_SUBSRC[@]}\" ':(exclude)lib/' ':(exclude)build/' ':(exclude).deps/' 2>/dev/null)\nif [ \"${#_SUBNEW[@]}\" -gt 0 ]; then\n  GIT_INDEX_FILE=\"$_SUBIDX\" git -C /app add -- \"${_SUBNEW[@]}\" 2>>\"$STDERR_LOG\" || true\nfi\nGIT_INDEX_FILE=\"$_SUBIDX\" git -C /app diff --binary --cached harbor_base \\\n  > \"$_SUB\" 2>>\"$STDERR_LOG\" || true\nrm -f \"$_SUBIDX\"\n\n# WHAT THE WHITELIST DROPPED, listed rather than silently discarded. The split above captures\n# every change to a TRACKED file (any extension, deletions included) but only NEW files that\n# look like source -- so an agent adding config.yml, a .cmake module or an extensionless script\n# loses it, and the patch still looks clean. That is the failure this manifest exists to expose.\n#\n# NEWEST FIRST, which is the useful ordering and not a cosmetic one: build output and image\n# assets are created before the agent phase starts, so anything the agent made sorts to the top.\n# Build artifacts are excluded by the same rules as the capture, to keep the list readable.\n#\n# If something here belongs in the submission, widen _SUBSRC. Do not reach for `add -A`.\n_SUBSKIP=/logs/verifier/submission-skipped.txt\n_SUBCAP=/tmp/.submission_captured\nif [ \"${#_SUBNEW[@]}\" -gt 0 ]; then printf '%s\\n' \"${_SUBNEW[@]}\" > \"$_SUBCAP\"; else : > \"$_SUBCAP\"; fi\n_SUBSKIPN=$(git -C /app ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- . \\\n    ':(exclude)lib/' ':(exclude)build/' ':(exclude).deps/' \\\n    ':(exclude)*.o' ':(exclude)*.d' ':(exclude)*.a' ':(exclude)*.lo' ':(exclude)*.la' \\\n    ':(exclude)*.so' ':(exclude)*.so.*' ':(exclude)*.gch' ':(exclude)*.pyc' 2>/dev/null \\\n  | grep -vxF -f \"$_SUBCAP\" 2>/dev/null | wc -l)\n{ echo \"# Untracked files NOT captured into submission.patch, newest first.\"\n  echo \"# Anything the agent created sorts above build output, which predates the agent phase.\"\n  echo \"# total: ${_SUBSKIPN}  (showing at most 40)\"\n  git -C /app ls-files --others --exclude-standard -- . \\\n      ':(exclude)lib/' ':(exclude)build/' ':(exclude).deps/' \\\n      ':(exclude)*.o' ':(exclude)*.d' ':(exclude)*.a' ':(exclude)*.lo' ':(exclude)*.la' \\\n      ':(exclude)*.so' ':(exclude)*.so.*' ':(exclude)*.gch' ':(exclude)*.pyc' 2>/dev/null \\\n    | grep -vxF -f \"$_SUBCAP\" 2>/dev/null | head -200 \\\n    | while IFS= read -r _f; do\n        printf '%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n' \"$(stat -c %Y \"/app/$_f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\" \\\n                              \"$(stat -c %s \"/app/$_f\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\" \"$_f\"\n      done \\\n    | sort -rn | head -40 | awk -F'\\t' '{printf \"%s\\t%s\\t%s\\n\",$1,$2,$3}'\n} > \"$_SUBSKIP\" 2>/dev/null || true\nrm -f \"$_SUBCAP\"\n\n# Size guard. The whitelist is a heuristic, so a task laying out source unusually could still\n# produce something enormous. -0,0 +1,414 @@\n+/**\n+ * Reads how a rendered grid header actually looks, for the header-row specs.\n+ *\n+ * jsdom loads no stylesheet, so a class name alone says nothing about the look. This helper builds the\n+ * page's real stylesheet the way the app ships it — the ui-kit and file-manager styles, then the app's own\n+ * `global.scss` compiled by Sass and Tailwind — and runs a small CSS cascade (layers, `!important`,\n+ * specificity, source order, inline styles, inheritance, `var()`) over the rendered DOM. A header passes\n+ * or fails on the look it ends up with, whether that comes from a utility class, an arbitrary value, an\n+ * app stylesheet rule, an `@utility`, a kit class or an inline style.\n+ *\n+ * ag-grid's own theme CSS is injected at runtime and is not present in jsdom; the one ag-grid rule the\n+ * header specs depend on (its right-aligned column type reverses the label row) is added by hand.\n+ */\n+import fs from 'node:fs';\n+import { createRequire } from 'node:module';\n+import path from 'node:path';\n+import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\n+\n+import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/postcss';\n+import postcss, { AtRule, ChildNode, Container, Rule } from 'postcss';\n+import * as sass from 'sass';\n+\n+const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);\n+const APP_DIR = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../../..');\n+\n+// ---- stylesheet ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+/** Inherited properties among the ones the specs read; custom properties inherit too. */\n+const INHERITED = new Set(['font-size', 'font-weight', 'text-transform', 'letter-spacing', 'text-align']);\n+const READ = new Set([...INHERITED, 'justify-content', 'flex-direction']);\n+\n+interface Declaration {\n+  value: string;\n+  important: boolean;\n+}\n+\n+interface StyleRule {\n+  selectors: string[];\n+  declarations: Map<string, Declaration>;\n+  /** Index of the cascade layer, or null when unlayered. */\n+  layer: number | null;\n+  order: number;\n+}\n+\n+export interface PageStyles {\n+  rules: StyleRule[];\n+  layerCount: number;\n+}\n+\n+const compileAppStylesheet = async (): Promise<string> => {\n+  const entry = path.join(APP_DIR, 'src/app/[lang]/global.scss');\n+  const compiled = sass.compile(entry, {\n+    importers: [\n+      {\n+        findFileUrl: (url: string) => (url.startsWith('@/') ? pathToFileURL(path.join(APP_DIR, url.slice(2))) : null),\n+      },\n+    ],\n+    silenceDeprecations: ['import'],\n+    logger: sass.Logger.silent,\n+  });\n+  const result = await postcss([tailwindcss({ base: APP_DIR })]).process(compiled.css, { from: entry });\n+  return result.css;\n+};\n+\n+/** Flattens nesting: `&` stands for the parent; a child without `&` is a descendant of it. */\n+const nestSelectors = (parents: string[], selectors: string[]): string[] => {\n+  if (parents.length === 0) return selectors;\n+  const parent = parents.length === 1 ? parents[0] : `:is(${parents.join(', ')})`;\n+  return selectors.map((selector) =>\n+    selector.includes('&') ? selector.replace(/&/g, `:is(${parent})`) : `:is(${parent}) ${selector}`,\n+  );\n+};\n+\n+const collectRules = (sheets: string[]): PageStyles => {\n+  const layers: string[] = [];\n+  const layerIndex = (name: string) => {\n+    if (!layers.includes(name)) layers.push(name);\n+    return layers.indexOf(name);\n+  };\n+  const rules: StyleRule[] = [];\n+  let order = 0;\n+  let anonymous = 0;\n+\n+  const walk = (container: Container<ChildNode>, parents: string[], layer: string | null) => {\n+    container.each((node) => {\n+      if (node.type === 'atrule') {\n+        const at = node as AtRule;\n+        if (at.name === 'layer') {\n+          if (!at.nodes) {\n+            at.params.split(',').forEach((name) => layerIndex(layer ? `${layer}.${name.trim()}` : name.trim()));\n+            return;\n+          }\n+          const name = at.params.trim() || `<anonymous-${anonymous++}>`;\n+          const full = layer ? `${layer}.${name}` : name;\n+          layerIndex(full);\n+          walk(at, parents, full);\n+        } else if (at.name === 'utility' && at.nodes) {\n+          // A user-defined utility applies like a class of the same name.\n+          walkRule(at, nestSelectors(parents, [`.${at.params.trim()}`]), layer);\n+        }\n+        // @media / @supports / @container / @keyframes / @property / @font-face do not apply here.\n+        return;\n+      }\n+      if (node.type === 'rule') walkRule(node as Rule, nestSelectors(parents, (node as Rule).selectors), layer);\n+    });\n+  };\n+\n+  const walkRule = (rule: Container<ChildNode>, selectors: string[], layer: string | null) => {\n+    const declarations = new Map<string, Declaration>();\n+    rule.each((child) => {\n+      if (child.type === 'decl' && (READ.has(child.prop) || child.prop.startsWith('--'))) {\n+        declarations.set(child.prop, { value: child.value.trim(), important: child.important });\n+      }\n+    });\n+    if (declarations.size > 0) {\n+      rules.push({ selectors, declarations, layer: layer === null ? null : layerIndex(layer), order: order++ });\n+    }\n+    walk(rule, selectors, layer);\n+  };\n+\n+  sheets.forEach((css) => walk(postcss.parse(css), [], null));\n+  return { rules, layerCount: layers.length };\n+};\n+\n+// ag-grid's right-aligned column type reverses the header label row (from ag-grid's own theme CSS).\n+const AG_GRID_RULES = '.ag-right-aligned-header .ag-header-cell-label { flex-direction: row-reverse; }';\n+\n+let pageStyles: Promise<PageStyles> | undefined;\n+\n+/** The page's stylesheet, in the order the app loads it. Built once per test file. */\n+export const loadPageStyles = (): Promise<PageStyles> => {\n+  pageStyles ??= (async () => {\n+    const read = (specifier: string) => fs.readFileSync(require.resolve(specifier), 'utf8');\n+    return collectRules([\n+      AG_GRID_RULES,\n+      read('@epam/ai-dial-ui-kit/styles.css'),\n+      read('@epam/ai-dial-react-file-manager/styles.css'),\n+      await compileAppStylesheet(),\n+    ]);\n+  })();\n+  return pageStyles;\n+};\n+\n+// ---- cascade ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+type Specificity = [number, number, number];\n+\n+const addSpecificity = (a: Specificity, b: Specificity): Specificity => [a[0] + b[0], a[1] + b[1], a[2] + b[2]];\n+const compareSpecificity = (a: Specificity, b: Specificity) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1] - b[1] || a[2] - b[2];\n+\n+const splitTopLevel = (list: string): string[] => {\n+  const parts: string[] = [];\n+  let depth = 0;\n+  let current = '';\n+  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {\n+    const char = list[i];\n+    if (char === '\\\\') {\n+      current += char + (list[i + 1] ?? '');\n+      i++;\n+      continue;\n+    }\n+    if (char === '(' || char === '[') depth++;\n+    if (char === ')' || char === ']') depth--;\n+    if (char === ',' && depth === 0) {\n+      parts.push(current);\n+      current = '';\n+    } else {\n+      current += char;\n+    }\n+  }\n+  return [...parts, current].map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n+};\n+\n+const specificity = (selector: string): Specificity => {\n+  let result: Specificity = [0, 0, 0];\n+  let i = 0;\n+  const readIdent = () => {\n+    while (i < selector.length && /[\\w\\-\\\\ -￿]/.test(selector[i])) i += selector[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1;\n+  };\n+  const readArgs = (): string => {\n+    let depth = 0;\n+    const start = i;\n+    for (; i < selector.length; i++) {\n+      if (selector[i] === '\\\\') {\n+        i++;\n+        continue;\n+      }\n+      if (selector[i] === '(') depth++;\n+      if (selector[i] === ')' && --depth === 0) {\n+        i++;\n+        break;\n+      }\n+    }\n+    return selector.slice(start + 1, i - 1);\n+  };\n+  while (i < selector.length) {\n+    const char = selector[i];\n+    if (char === '#') {\n+      i++;\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [1, 0, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === '.') {\n+      i++;\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === '[') {\n+      while (i < selector.length && selector[i] !== ']') i += selector[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1;\n+      i++;\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === ':') {\n+      const element = selector[i + 1] === ':';\n+      i += element ? 2 : 1;\n+      const start = i;\n+      readIdent();\n+      const name = selector.slice(start, i).toLowerCase();\n+      const args = selector[i] === '(' ? readArgs() : null;\n+      if (element) {\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 0, 1]);\n+      } else if (name === 'where') {\n+        // no specificity\n+      } else (compareSpecificity(next, max) > 0 ? next : max), [0, 0, 0]);\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, most);\n+      } else {\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+      }\n+    } else if (/[a-zA-Z]/.test(char)) {\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 0, 1]);\n+    } else {\n+      i++;\n+    }\n+  }\n+  return result;\n+};\n+\n+interface Candidate -0,0 +1,414 @@\n+/**\n+ * Reads how a rendered grid header actually looks, for the header-row specs.\n+ *\n+ * jsdom loads no stylesheet, so a class name alone says nothing about the look. This helper builds the\n+ * page's real stylesheet the way the app ships it — the ui-kit and file-manager styles, then the app's own\n+ * `global.scss` compiled by Sass and Tailwind — and runs a small CSS cascade (layers, `!important`,\n+ * specificity, source order, inline styles, inheritance, `var()`) over the rendered DOM. A header passes\n+ * or fails on the look it ends up with, whether that comes from a utility class, an arbitrary value, an\n+ * app stylesheet rule, an `@utility`, a kit class or an inline style.\n+ *\n+ * ag-grid's own theme CSS is injected at runtime and is not present in jsdom; the one ag-grid rule the\n+ * header specs depend on (its right-aligned column type reverses the label row) is added by hand.\n+ */\n+import fs from 'node:fs';\n+import { createRequire } from 'node:module';\n+import path from 'node:path';\n+import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';\n+\n+import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/postcss';\n+import postcss, { AtRule, ChildNode, Container, Rule } from 'postcss';\n+import * as sass from 'sass';\n+\n+const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);\n+const APP_DIR = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '../../../..');\n+\n+// ---- stylesheet ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+/** Inherited properties among the ones the specs read; custom properties inherit too. */\n+const INHERITED = new Set(['font-size', 'font-weight', 'text-transform', 'letter-spacing', 'text-align']);\n+const READ = new Set([...INHERITED, 'justify-content', 'flex-direction']);\n+\n+interface Declaration {\n+  value: string;\n+  important: boolean;\n+}\n+\n+interface StyleRule {\n+  selectors: string[];\n+  declarations: Map<string, Declaration>;\n+  /** Index of the cascade layer, or null when unlayered. */\n+  layer: number | null;\n+  order: number;\n+}\n+\n+export interface PageStyles {\n+  rules: StyleRule[];\n+  layerCount: number;\n+}\n+\n+const compileAppStylesheet = async (): Promise<string> => {\n+  const entry = path.join(APP_DIR, 'src/app/[lang]/global.scss');\n+  const compiled = sass.compile(entry, {\n+    importers: [\n+      {\n+        findFileUrl: (url: string) => (url.startsWith('@/') ? pathToFileURL(path.join(APP_DIR, url.slice(2))) : null),\n+      },\n+    ],\n+    silenceDeprecations: ['import'],\n+    logger: sass.Logger.silent,\n+  });\n+  const result = await postcss([tailwindcss({ base: APP_DIR })]).process(compiled.css, { from: entry });\n+  return result.css;\n+};\n+\n+/** Flattens nesting: `&` stands for the parent; a child without `&` is a descendant of it. */\n+const nestSelectors = (parents: string[], selectors: string[]): string[] => {\n+  if (parents.length === 0) return selectors;\n+  const parent = parents.length === 1 ? parents[0] : `:is(${parents.join(', ')})`;\n+  return selectors.map((selector) =>\n+    selector.includes('&') ? selector.replace(/&/g, `:is(${parent})`) : `:is(${parent}) ${selector}`,\n+  );\n+};\n+\n+const collectRules = (sheets: string[]): PageStyles => {\n+  const layers: string[] = [];\n+  const layerIndex = (name: string) => {\n+    if (!layers.includes(name)) layers.push(name);\n+    return layers.indexOf(name);\n+  };\n+  const rules: StyleRule[] = [];\n+  let order = 0;\n+  let anonymous = 0;\n+\n+  const walk = (container: Container<ChildNode>, parents: string[], layer: string | null) => {\n+    container.each((node) => {\n+      if (node.type === 'atrule') {\n+        const at = node as AtRule;\n+        if (at.name === 'layer') {\n+          if (!at.nodes) {\n+            at.params.split(',').forEach((name) => layerIndex(layer ? `${layer}.${name.trim()}` : name.trim()));\n+            return;\n+          }\n+          const name = at.params.trim() || `<anonymous-${anonymous++}>`;\n+          const full = layer ? `${layer}.${name}` : name;\n+          layerIndex(full);\n+          walk(at, parents, full);\n+        } else if (at.name === 'utility' && at.nodes) {\n+          // A user-defined utility applies like a class of the same name.\n+          walkRule(at, nestSelectors(parents, [`.${at.params.trim()}`]), layer);\n+        }\n+        // @media / @supports / @container / @keyframes / @property / @font-face do not apply here.\n+        return;\n+      }\n+      if (node.type === 'rule') walkRule(node as Rule, nestSelectors(parents, (node as Rule).selectors), layer);\n+    });\n+  };\n+\n+  const walkRule = (rule: Container<ChildNode>, selectors: string[], layer: string | null) => {\n+    const declarations = new Map<string, Declaration>();\n+    rule.each((child) => {\n+      if (child.type === 'decl' && (READ.has(child.prop) || child.prop.startsWith('--'))) {\n+        declarations.set(child.prop, { value: child.value.trim(), important: child.important });\n+      }\n+    });\n+    if (declarations.size > 0) {\n+      rules.push({ selectors, declarations, layer: layer === null ? null : layerIndex(layer), order: order++ });\n+    }\n+    walk(rule, selectors, layer);\n+  };\n+\n+  sheets.forEach((css) => walk(postcss.parse(css), [], null));\n+  return { rules, layerCount: layers.length };\n+};\n+\n+// ag-grid's right-aligned column type reverses the header label row (from ag-grid's own theme CSS).\n+const AG_GRID_RULES = '.ag-right-aligned-header .ag-header-cell-label { flex-direction: row-reverse; }';\n+\n+let pageStyles: Promise<PageStyles> | undefined;\n+\n+/** The page's stylesheet, in the order the app loads it. Built once per test file. */\n+export const loadPageStyles = (): Promise<PageStyles> => {\n+  pageStyles ??= (async () => {\n+    const read = (specifier: string) => fs.readFileSync(require.resolve(specifier), 'utf8');\n+    return collectRules([\n+      AG_GRID_RULES,\n+      read('@epam/ai-dial-ui-kit/styles.css'),\n+      read('@epam/ai-dial-react-file-manager/styles.css'),\n+      await compileAppStylesheet(),\n+    ]);\n+  })();\n+  return pageStyles;\n+};\n+\n+// ---- cascade ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n+\n+type Specificity = [number, number, number];\n+\n+const addSpecificity = (a: Specificity, b: Specificity): Specificity => [a[0] + b[0], a[1] + b[1], a[2] + b[2]];\n+const compareSpecificity = (a: Specificity, b: Specificity) => a[0] - b[0] || a[1] - b[1] || a[2] - b[2];\n+\n+const splitTopLevel = (list: string): string[] => {\n+  const parts: string[] = [];\n+  let depth = 0;\n+  let current = '';\n+  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {\n+    const char = list[i];\n+    if (char === '\\\\') {\n+      current += char + (list[i + 1] ?? '');\n+      i++;\n+      continue;\n+    }\n+    if (char === '(' || char === '[') depth++;\n+    if (char === ')' || char === ']') depth--;\n+    if (char === ',' && depth === 0) {\n+      parts.push(current);\n+      current = '';\n+    } else {\n+      current += char;\n+    }\n+  }\n+  return [...parts, current].map((part) => part.trim()).filter(Boolean);\n+};\n+\n+const specificity = (selector: string): Specificity => {\n+  let result: Specificity = [0, 0, 0];\n+  let i = 0;\n+  const readIdent = () => {\n+    while (i < selector.length && /[\\w\\-\\\\ -￿]/.test(selector[i])) i += selector[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1;\n+  };\n+  const readArgs = (): string => {\n+    let depth = 0;\n+    const start = i;\n+    for (; i < selector.length; i++) {\n+      if (selector[i] === '\\\\') {\n+        i++;\n+        continue;\n+      }\n+      if (selector[i] === '(') depth++;\n+      if (selector[i] === ')' && --depth === 0) {\n+        i++;\n+        break;\n+      }\n+    }\n+    return selector.slice(start + 1, i - 1);\n+  };\n+  while (i < selector.length) {\n+    const char = selector[i];\n+    if (char === '#') {\n+      i++;\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [1, 0, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === '.') {\n+      i++;\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === '[') {\n+      while (i < selector.length && selector[i] !== ']') i += selector[i] === '\\\\' ? 2 : 1;\n+      i++;\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+    } else if (char === ':') {\n+      const element = selector[i + 1] === ':';\n+      i += element ? 2 : 1;\n+      const start = i;\n+      readIdent();\n+      const name = selector.slice(start, i).toLowerCase();\n+      const args = selector[i] === '(' ? readArgs() : null;\n+      if (element) {\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 0, 1]);\n+      } else if (name === 'where') {\n+        // no specificity\n+      } else if (['is', 'not', 'has', 'matches'].includes(name) && args !== null) {\n+        const most = splitTopLevel(args)\n+          .map(specificity)\n+          .reduce<Specificity>((max, next) => (compareSpecificity(next, max) > 0 ? next : max), [0, 0, 0]);\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, most);\n+      } else {\n+        result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 1, 0]);\n+      }\n+    } else if (/[a-zA-Z]/.test(char)) {\n+      readIdent();\n+      result = addSpecificity(result, [0, 0, 1]);\n+    } else {\n+      i++;\n+    }\n+  }\n+  return result;\n+};\n+\n+interface Candidate extends Declaration {\n+  /** Cascade rank, compared left to right; higher wins. */\n+  rank: number[];\n+}\n+\n+const matchingSpecificity = (element: Element, selectors: string[]): Specificity | null => {","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_vrtx_01W3BFZ9v8kH2WJpkBqHX1Uf","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}
